% =====================================================================
\section{The angular integrals}\label{app:angular}
For $n=1$, long division gives
$u^3/(1+xu)=u^2/x-u/x^2+1/x^3-1/[x^3(1+xu)]$, and term by term
$\int_0^1=1/3x-1/2x^2+1/x^3-\ln(1+x)/x^4$; then $\sigma_1=3x^2\int_0^1u^3/(1+xu)\,du$ gives
\eqref{eq:mu1}. For $n=3$, $1-\mu_3=3\int_0^1u^2(1+xu/3)^{-3}du$; with $a=x/3$ and $w=1+au$,
$\int_0^1u^2(1+au)^{-3}du=a^{-3}\int_1^{1+a}(w-1)^2w^{-3}dw=a^{-3}[\ln w+2/w-1/2w^2]_1^{1+a}$,
which gives~\eqref{eq:mu3}.

% =====================================================================
\section{Verification and data}\label{app:verify}
The companion script \texttt{trsd\_v8\_verify.py} reproduces every number (the rotation-curve
likelihood with \texttt{--likelihood} and the shape profiles of \S14.3 and \S17 with
\texttt{sparc\_likelihood.py --shapes}, the running exponent with \texttt{--running}, through the module \texttt{sparc\_likelihood.py} and the
SPARC tables \texttt{SPARC\_Lelli2016c.txt} and \texttt{MassModels\_Lelli2016c.txt}):
(1) the identities---the perfect-square potential, the relaxation flow and the
reconciliation of Theorem~\ref{thm:flow}, the couplings~\eqref{eq:couplings}, the physical
quartic and $g_\chi$, $\mathcal F'=\mu$, the information--energy relation,
$\rho_cc^2=\pi^3\az^2/8G$, $F=c^4/4G$, $m_U$, $M_{\rm Hubble}=4M_{\rm total}$;
(2) the coil crossings; (3) the family $\mu_n$ with high-order series; (4) the SPARC fits;
(5) the planetary tails; (6) the phantom profile and $r_*$; (7) the dark sector;
(8) the horizon numbers; (9) the CMB pattern and cosmic variance; (10) the budget and the CAMB
fit. The RAR file is the 2696-point SPARC set of \cite{McGaugh2016}
($\gbar$, $g_{\rm obs}$ and errors in $10^{-10}$ m\,s$^{-2}$, galaxy index), as distributed
with the RARinterpret repository; the script expects it at \texttt{data/RAR.dat}. Fits
minimize the sum of squared $\log_{10}$ residuals; statistical errors come from a galaxy
bootstrap with 200 draws and seed 20260925.

\section{Archived constructions}\label{app:archive}
The following draft 1 constructions are withdrawn from the framework, each for the reason given;
they are recorded so that nothing is lost.
\begin{enumerate}\itemsep1pt
\item \emph{The Lissajous chain for the threefold count.} Two equal-frequency perpendicular
oscillators with a $90^\circ$ phase offset trace a circle, not a trefoil. The count follows
from the crossing window $2\le\omega<3$ and Postulate~\ref{post:gen} (\S2.3). Release 1.4.0 restored at the opening of
Section~\ref{sec:axiom} the sentence ``The threefold count is the Lissajous figure of the two counter-rotating arcs: two
equal-frequency perpendicular motions a quarter turn apart trace on the sphere a closed figure with three nodes at $2\pi/3$, where
the counter-rotation leaves no net tangential motion and the curvature concentrates.'' Release 1.6.0 corrects it: on the sphere,
two semicircles counter-turning at one rate about perpendicular axes cross on a great circle if started together and on an arc if
started a quarter turn apart, with no node, and a point carried by both traces a figure-eight with at most one node; the $2\pi/3$
spacing is the beat of the fold against the conjugate lobe (\S2.4).
\item \emph{The Torridity canonical scale} $2^{-1/4}\az$, argued from which cascade ratio has a
terminating decimal expansion. Decimal termination depends on the base, and the coincidence
$2^{-1/4}\az\simeq(\pi/3)\times10^{-10}\ \mathrm{m\,s^{-2}}$ depends on the SI units.
\item \emph{The quantized sequences} $(\pi/3)(k\pm1)/k\times10^{-10}\ \mathrm{m\,s^{-2}}$, the
dwarf scale $(2\pi^{\pi/3}/3+\tfrac1{12})\times10^{-10}$ and the cluster scale
$(4\pi/3)\times10^{-10}$, with falsifiers F4--F6: each multiplies a pure number by a unit.
A dwarf or cluster scale would have to enter as a derived multiple of $\az$.
\item \emph{The screening factor} $(1+x)^{-\pi/2}$: it changes the deep slope to $\tfrac34+\tfrac\pi2$
and misfits SPARC by $0.17$ dex (\S15).
\item \emph{The two-direction test} of $\az$--$H_0$ at $0.056\%$ and $0.04\%$: one relation read
twice, compared at a precision the data do not have (\S14.2).
\item \emph{$G$ from the cell count to $0.001\%$}: $N$ was computed from $G$ (\S24.2).
\item \emph{The horizon resonance ring}: the phantom profile has no maximum (\S24.7).
\item \emph{Octupole modulus handedness}: impossible for a real sky (\S25.3).
\item \emph{The draft-1 self-coupling} $3\hbar^3/2\mdelta^2c$ and \emph{the vacuum constant} $V_0=2$ as dark
energy (\S21).
\item \emph{The eikonal replacement} $\Box\to d/ds$; the same flow survives as relaxation (\S7).
\item \emph{The three-arc law as the adopted completion} (draft 2): the rotation-curve likelihood
places $n=3$ at $\Delta\chi^2/S^2=18.6$ from the optimum (\S14.3).
\item \emph{The quantized values} of draft 1's exponent quantization: $2\pi/3$ for disk galaxies (a
fixed $2\pi/3$ lies at $\Delta\chi^2/S^2=397$, and disks read $m=1.03$--$1.28$). The regime
dependence itself is kept: it is the running exponent of \S17.
\item \emph{The two-stage reading} (draft 3): $n=2$ as the two lobes of the conjugate pair. $n$ is a
tail order, not a count, and $n=2$ lies $2.3\sigma$ outside the galaxy window (\S14.4).
\end{enumerate}

\section{Changes}\label{app:changes}
\subsection*{Release 1.16.2}
Patch release: specifies the local Auxoviscausal strain determinant and its sign-consistent evolution; gives the local Bullet Cluster strain-contrast calculation and data requirements; and adds the covariant matter/radiation/thermodynamic conservation and perturbation closure. No new field, force, or fitted parameter is introduced. A numerical Bullet Cluster map and a full cosmological perturbation integration remain explicit computational tasks.


\emph{Versioning.} Releases are numbered MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH. MAJOR changes an axiom or the physical reading
of a sector, so that some result changes meaning; MINOR adds derived results or predictions without changing
existing ones; PATCH corrects errors, updates numbers to new data, or changes presentation. Version 1.0.0 is
the first release; the drafts that preceded it are recorded below, unabridged.

\subsection*{Release 1.15.1}
Patch release: derives the conditional Auxoviscausal junction rule from the spatial-volume determinant of the existing conformal metric. The divergent physical strain is not matched directly; the finite remainder $\widehat{\mathcal A}_{\rm aux}=\frac13\ln(\hat V/V_*)$ matches if the conformal spatial metric and identified flow tube remain regular across the boundary. In homogeneous FLRW this remainder carries no independent record of the previous aeon’s total $\ln a$. No new field or independent transmission coefficient is added.

\subsection*{Release 1.14.0}
Minor release: derives Auxoviscausality as the covariant retained logarithmic proper-volume strain of the existing phase-flow congruence. The volume determinant gives $D\mathcal A_{\rm aux}=\vartheta/3$; integration along a phase-flow worldline gives the causal memory integral; Raychaudhuri and the Einstein source give the local Ricci-load response, including the shear correction. The FLRW limit reduces to $\mathcal A_{\rm aux}=\ln(a/a_0)$ and links its rate to the horizon information density. No new field or coupling is added. The geometric memory law is closed; microscopic yield-threshold plasticity and dissipative entropy production are explicitly not claimed.

\subsection*{Release 1.13.0}
Minor release: Section~\ref{sec:chirp-recoil} derives the asymmetric gas/phase response at collision, the relaxation-centroid velocity from $f(t)$, the mass-conserving local transfer flux, proper-volume recoil from the congruence expansion scalar, its Einstein/Raychaudhuri source, the dilation-derived recoil $v_{\rm rec}=-R\dot\Phi/c^2$ and its retarded-current integral, the domain-average expansion relation, and the horizon identities $H\propto\iota_H$ and $V_H\propto\iota_H^{-3}$. No external streaming velocity or new force is added. The profile-derived flux must satisfy the existing geodesic phase equation; the sign of local volume change follows the metric solution rather than being imposed.

\subsection*{Release 1.12.0}
Minor release: the mergers of \S21.7 are worked in a new \S15.3 (GRUT release 1.19.0, \S47.9); nothing earlier changes. The phase equation has no baryonic source and the flow is geodesic;
in the unsettled state the cross term between two clusters is superposition, and the nonlinear cross term of the support law enters through the settled share $f$ alone; the relaxation
clock restarts at core passage, $f=0.0048$ at the Bullet Cluster and at most $0.031$ ($1$ Gyr) and $0.062$ ($2$ Gyr); each peak carries its own cluster's share. On synthetic maps a collision
separates an excess on the galaxies from a law computed on the baryons present ($T=97.7$) and excludes a swapped assignment ($T=99.1$), and does not separate TRSD from particle dark matter
($T=-0.08$). Not adopted: the cross assignment of the condensate, and the Abell~520 dark core as a prediction (it is the test of row 42). Register rows 41--42; eight references added; the GRUT
reference updated to its release 1.19.0. A plain-English edition accompanies this release.

\subsection*{Release 1.11.0}
Minor release: \S30 completes the unification. The coil's features write one action, the axiom with the rotation held at its rate, the
Yang--Mills terms of $(SU(3)\times SU(2)\times U(1))/\mathbb Z_6$, three generations of fermions and one Higgs doublet (GRUT \S54.5); its content
fixes the running, $b_3=-7$, $b_2=-\tfrac{19}6$, $b_Y=\tfrac{41}6$, so the triangle's coupling closes in strong coupling at the nucleon and the
phase and the pair are one electroweak coupling (GRUT \S54.6); the horizon and the nucleon are transmutations from one Planck cell of the
electric and the strong coupling, and with H4 the strong datum is $K=19.7566\pm0.0002$. Correction: electromagnetism is the phase of the
three crossings, acting on the fermions; the phase $\theta$ of $\phi_\pm$ is the global phase of the rotation and carries no gauge field.

\subsection*{Release 1.10.2}
Patch release: \S30 pointed to GRUT 1.17.2; it is replaced by the completed \S30 of release 1.11.0.

\subsection*{Release 1.10.1}
Minor release. \S24.18 (new): the present in Planck cells, $\ln(R_H/\lP)=1/\alpha+\ln8\pi+\tfrac{3\pi}2\alpha$ (H4 of GRUT \S48.12), read as a
selection of the epoch, not a law for $\alpha$ (as a law it would make $\alpha$ drift $10^5$ times faster than atomic clocks allow); it gives
$H_0=67.3765$ with no cosmological input, $0.4\sigma$ from the CMB fit, and $\Lambda\lP^2$ as $e^{-2/\alpha}$. \S30 (new): the four interactions
from the coil, the gauge group $(SU(3)\times SU(2)\times U(1))/\mathbb Z_6$ from its $\mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_3$, the hierarchy
$Gm_p^2/\hbar c=K^2(16\pi)^{-2/3}e^{-2/3\alpha-\pi\alpha}$, and no unification scale, so no gauge-mediated proton decay. Register rows 38--40.

\subsection*{Release 1.10.0}
A consolidation release; no axiom, reading or result changes. \emph{Consistency:} every current number is quoted at the resonance budget of
\S24.16, refitted reproducibly to Planck's measured acoustic angle $100\theta_*=1.04110\pm0.00031$: $H_0=67.35\pm0.06$, $\omega_b=0.02248$,
$\omega_c=0.12022$ (release 1.9.0 quoted $67.33$, $0.02250$, $0.12031$, which were not one consistent point); the numerical-values appendix has one
column, the variance analysis is redone at the resonance budget, and the register rows that still carried the withdrawn $5/2$ budget
(matter fraction, Hubble constant, freeze, finite aeon, first starlight, baryon density) are updated. \emph{The Hubble constant} is stated as
the framework's prediction: one expansion rate, $67.35$, which the dilation cannot move (clocks and rulers shift by $|\Phi|/c^2\lesssim10^{-5}$) and
a local void cannot carry (the supernova Hubble diagram), so distance ladders calibrated alike converge on $67.3$ (\S21.7, register row~7).
\emph{Presentation:} a one-page abstract; release narration removed from the running text; ``new tags removed. A plain-English edition
accompanies this release.

\subsection*{Release 1.9.0}
The dark sector as one geometry, formalizing a proposal made for this release. No axiom, reading or earlier result changes---the vacuum
stays the horizon's capacity and the cosmic share the rotation held at its rate, now shown to be the same quanta in two states---so the
release is a minor one.
\begin{enumerate}\itemsep1pt
\item \S21.7 (new): dark energy is spacetime unexcited, the spin's quanta held by the final horizon, $n_*\hbar H_\infty=\rho_\Lambda c^2$ with
$n_*=3/8\pi\lP^2R_\infty$, the capacity of \S24.11 counted in quanta; what is called dark matter is spacetime excited, the same quanta carried by
the rotation; one stress tensor, $\hbar H_\infty(n\,u_\mu u_\nu-n_*g_{\mu\nu})$, whose vacuum term is the generator-defect tension over the square of
the final radius, $2\lambda_*=\fd^2(\pi)/\dgen$. $w=-1+\Theta$ with $\Theta$ the excited fraction, equality at $z=0.372$; the threshold is the vacuum's
own density, at the cosmic ratio a baryon density of $0.65\ \mathrm{m^{-3}}$. The states do not convert, the vacuum is $0.34\%$ of a halo, and the
background expansion is unchanged; the inventory is spacetime, $0.949$, and baryons, $0.050$.
\item \S21.7: the dilation. The mass lensing infers is the baryonic mass times $\mathcal S=1/\mu=1/\cos_n\theta$, $\tan\theta=\az/g$, exact with the
dynamic exponent and $g^2=\gbar(g^n+\az^n)^{1/n}$; at $n=2$ it is $1/\cos\theta$, the proposal's form, whose $2$, $3.86$, $11.5$ fall at $g=0.58$, $0.27$,
$0.087\,\az$; $\mathcal S\to1$ at the centre and $r/\ell$ toward the edge, $4$ at $39$ kpc and $11$ at $116$ kpc around $10^{11}M_\odot$; set by the
horizon, $\az\propto\sqrt\Lambda$ at the present; varying with capture and relaxation (register row 33); its excess carried by the excited
geometry as the phantom density; how far it grows open (register row 34). The sky's slant $r/\sqrt{r^2-b^2}$ keeps the total; the closure's
$M_{\rm Hubble}/M_{\rm total}=4$ is Cauchy's ratio; the Bullet Cluster's excess passed through with the galaxies, carried by the collisionless
excitation. The heights of the proposal's first table and its single mean $\langle\mathcal S\rangle=4$ corrected.
\item \S21.7: the proposal's second form. The dilation field $S=e^{-\psi}\simeq1-2\Phi/c^2$ is the refractive index of the optical metric, and
$\mathcal S=\partial_r\ln S/\partial_r\ln S_b$; the field equation is~\eqref{eq:aqual} in $\ln S$, its stiffness set by $R_{\az}|\nabla\ln S|=g/\az$, and the
screening scale is the horizon's, $1/R_\infty$ (register row 35); the density switch is the capture fraction, $n=3/2$, $\alpha_0=1$,
$\rho_c=3\sigma^2/4\pi GR^2$, gating the excess; the background's consistency condition is the closure law, and the middle scales are geometric
means, $\ell_P^2=\tfrac12r_s\lambda_C$, $\ell^2=r_sR_{\az}$, $g^2=\gbar\az$. Corrected: the excess assigned to the value of $S$ rather than its gradient,
a coupling rising with density and multiplying the baryons, and the enclosed-mass form of $\ln S$ inside a source.
\item \S21.7: the dilation on the unbound web. The background cannot move; the rate measured inside a region of contrast $\delta$ growing at $f$
is $H(1-f\delta/3)$. The field cannot separate scales, and a dilation set by it alone is excluded by $S_8$ ($6.4\sigma$ against DES Y6). The
dilation keeps a clock's causes: it reaches the unbound web only where the horizon sets the clocks, mean density below $2\rho_\Lambda$, from the
onset of acceleration ($z=0.630$), and holds where the well outweighs all of the medium's motion, above $68$ Mpc at the fixed point
$\sigma_{1D}=494\ \mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$, with no input. Being the horizon's, it is a conformal factor: light crosses it undilated, and the late
Sachs--Wolfe signal falls ($\times0.28$--$0.71$ at $\ell=2$--$20$; $\times4{,}200$ had light seen it). $S_8$, shear and CMB lensing keep the
background's values, the Cosmicflows-4 bulk flows fall at the $78$th and $90$th percentiles, and the ladder's $H_0$ is not reached: a local
void would put an $8.3\%$ step in the supernova Hubble diagram, which shows none to $0.60\%$. The earlier forms of this release, which held
the test run's tensions unchanged and then read the ladder's $H_0$ as the void's rate, corrected.
\item Terminology: the proposal's word ``stretch'' is replaced by its physics term, the dilation: $S\simeq1-2\Phi/c^2$ is spacetime's dilation
as light measures it, half from time and half from space, and $\mathcal S$ its gradient against the baryons' (\S21.7). The cells of \S24.9,
which do not stretch, keep their word.
\item Title, abstract, contributions, \S0.6, the \S21 reading note, the \S23 table, \S27, conclusions, register rows 30--37 and numerical values
updated; twenty-one references added, and the GRUT reference updated to its release 1.16.0.
\end{enumerate}
